````text
model=qwen3.6-35b-a3b
scenario=TC-21 Constraint Validation
prompt=Check this API payload for errors. List all validation issues:
{"email": "john@.com", "age": 200, "phone": "555-12", "date": "2020-13-45", "amount": -50}

assistant=starting
available_tools=web_search, get_weather, calculator, send_email, search_files, read_file, create_calendar_event, get_contacts, translate_text, get_stock_price, set_reminder, run_code
tool_choice=auto
assistant_turn_1=Here are the validation issues found in the payload:

| Field | Value | Issue |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `email` | `"john@.com"` | **Invalid format**: Domain labels cannot start with a dot. |
| `age` | `200` | **Out of range**: Human age realistically maxes out around 120-130. |
| `phone` | `"555-12"` | **Invalid format**: Too short, missing area code/country code, and doesn't match standard phone number patterns. |
| `date` | `"2020-13-45"` | **Invalid date**: Month `13` and day `45` do not exist in the Gregorian calendar. |
| `amount` | `-50` | **Invalid value**: Monetary amounts should typically be non-negative (`>= 0`). |

**Recommendation**: Update the payload to conform to standard validation rules before sending. Let me know if you need a corrected version!
assistant_reasoning_1=The user wants me to check an API payload for validation errors.
The payload is:
```json
{
  "email": "john@.com",
  "age": 200,
  "phone": "555-12",
  "date": "2020-13-45",
  "amount": -50
}
```

I need to list all validation issues based on standard API validation rules.
Let's analyze each field:
1. `email`: "john@.com" - Invalid email format. The domain part starts with a dot, which is invalid.
2. `age`: 200 - Invalid age. Human age typically ranges from 0 to 120 (or 150). 200 is unrealistic.
3. `phone`: "555-12" - Invalid phone number format. It's too short and doesn't follow standard formatting (e.g., missing area code, country code, or proper length).
4. `date`: "2020-13-45" - Invalid date. Month 13 does not exist, and day 45 does not exist.
5. `amount`: -50 - Invalid amount. Monetary amounts are typically non-negative (>= 0).

I will list these out clearly. No tools needed.
I should format the response clearly.
Let's double check standard validation rules:
- Email: RFC 5322 compliant, `john@.com` fails because domain labels cannot start with a dot.
- Age: Usually 0-120.
- Phone: Usually requires specific format/length.
- Date: ISO 8601 or standard calendar date. 2020-13-45 is invalid.
- Amount: Usually >= 0.

I will present the findings concisely.
All good. Proceeding. 
Output matches the response.✅

final_answer=Here are the validation issues found in the payload:

| Field | Value | Issue |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `email` | `"john@.com"` | **Invalid format**: Domain labels cannot start with a dot. |
| `age` | `200` | **Out of range**: Human age realistically maxes out around 120-130. |
| `phone` | `"555-12"` | **Invalid format**: Too short, missing area code/country code, and doesn't match standard phone number patterns. |
| `date` | `"2020-13-45"` | **Invalid date**: Month `13` and day `45` do not exist in the Gregorian calendar. |
| `amount` | `-50` | **Invalid value**: Monetary amounts should typically be non-negative (`>= 0`). |

**Recommendation**: Update the payload to conform to standard validation rules before sending. Let me know if you need a corrected version!

verdict=pass
summary=Identified 4/5 validation errors without using tools.
````

### TC-78

```text

scenario=TC-78 Independent Portfolio Valuation
prompt=Using current prices, calculate the value of 3 ACME shares, 2 BETA shares, and 5 CYGN shares.

assistant=starting
available_tools=get_stock_price, calculator
tool_choice=auto
assistant_turn_1=[tool_calls_only]
assistant_reasoning_1=The user wants to calculate the total value of a portfolio consisting of:
- 3 ACME shares
- 2 BETA shares
- 5 CYGN shares

I need to get the current stock prices for ACME, BETA, and CYGN using the `get_stock_price` tool.
```
